## 任务：综合状态检查（Seed 7 + Seed 5 eval + Seed 4）

### 子任务 1: Seed 7 GPU 内存和进度（westd cuda:0,1）
SSH 到 westd（westd-47453），ssh_read_only：
```bash
# GPU 内存 — 关注 GPU 0,1 是否 OOM 风险
nvidia-smi --query-gpu=index,memory.used,memory.total,utilization.gpu --format=csv,noheader

# Seed 7 进程存活
ps -p 226699 -o pid,etime --no-headers 2>/dev/null && echo "SEED7 ALIVE" || echo "SEED7 DEAD"

# 最近日志 — 看当前 step 和是否有 OOM/error
tail -50 ~/runs/cost-verify-agent-rl/exp029_seed7.log 2>/dev/null

# 检查是否通过 step 5 backward pass（关键 OOM 风险点）
grep -i "step.*5\|backward\|oom\|error\|killed" ~/runs/cost-verify-agent-rl/exp029_seed7.log 2>/dev/null | tail -20
```

如果 Seed 7 已 OOM 或 dead，立即报告！

### 子任务 2: Seed 5 OOD eval 状态（westd cuda:3）
```bash

The desk is a likely location. The goal is to restore the cube so that each of the faces consists of a single, unique color.\\nAvailable actions use standard Singmaster notation for face rotations: U, U', D, D', L, L', R, R', F, F', B, B'.\\n- Faces: U (Up), D (Down), L (Left), R (Right), F (Front), B (Back).\\n- Modifiers: A letter alone means 90° clockwise (e.g., 'R').
